+def legacy_execution_binding(record):
+    """Historical read-only projection; never a current binding or DCP policy."""
+    if record.get('schema_version')=='control_tower.execution_binding.v1': return record
+    selected=record['selection']; audit=record['audit']; fallback=record.get('fallback',{})
+    lane=record['risk_lane']; required=audit['required']
+    return {
+        'schema_version':'control_tower.execution_binding.v1',
+        'packet_id':record['packet_id'],'stage':record['stage'],'authority':'NONE',
+        'authority_refs':{'task_packet_ref':'UNKNOWN','operator_gate_refs':[]},
+        'policy_refs':{'dcp_route_decision_ref':'NOT_RUN','dcp_status':'NOT_RUN','dcp_risk_class':'NOT_RUN',
+            'task_orchestrator_state_ref':'NOT_RUN','universal_router_recommendation_ref':'NOT_RUN',
+            'audit_broker_certification_ref':'NOT_RUN','context_packet_ref':'NOT_RUN'},
+        'delivery':{'risk_lane':lane},
+        'selection':{key:selected[key] for key in ('runner','runner_availability','model','effort')} | {
+            'connector':'UNKNOWN','configured_identity':'UNKNOWN','response_claimed_identity':'UNKNOWN',
+            'provider_attested_identity':'UNKNOWN','containment_evidence':[]},
+        'constraints':{'allowed_actions':[],'forbidden_actions':['merge','activate'],
+            'stop_conditions':[],'custody_constraints':[],'context_constraints':[]},
+        'justification':record['justification'],
+        'audit':{'effective_required':required,
+            'effective_requirement_sources':[f'delivery:{lane}'] if lane in {'L2','L3'} else (['legacy:required'] if required else []),
+            'upstream_obligations':[],'selected_auditor_binding':'NOT_RUN'},
+        'fallback':{'allowed':bool(fallback.get('runner') and fallback.get('model')),
+            'trigger':fallback.get('trigger') or 'NOT_RUN',
+            'binding':{'runner':fallback.get('runner') or 'NOT_RUN','model':fallback.get('model') or 'NOT_RUN','effort':'UNKNOWN'}},
+        'compatibility':{'legacy_source':'control_tower.routing_record.v1','legacy_record':record},
+    }
+
+
+def governed_schema_errors(value,kind,repo):
+    """Validate only the repo-owned GEC schema set, offline and fail-closed."""
+    try:
+        from jsonschema import Draft7Validator
+        from referencing import Registry, Resource
+        from referencing.jsonschema import DRAFT7
+        directory=Path(repo)/'schemas'/'governed_execution'
+        schemas=[_load_json(path) for path in sorted(directory.glob('*.schema.json'))]
+        resources=[]
+        for schema in schemas:
+            Draft7Validator.check_schema(schema)
+            resources.append((schema['$id'],Resource(contents=schema,specification=DRAFT7)))
+        registry=Registry().with_resources(resources)
+        schema=_load_json(directory/(kind+'.schema.json'))
+        validator=Draft7Validator(schema,registry=registry)
+        return sorted(f'{error.json_path}: {error.message}' for error in validator.iter_errors(value))
+    except Exception as exc:
+        return [f'GEC_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE: {type(exc).__name__}']
+
+
+def validate_binding_obj(binding,repo=None,*,legacy_read_only=False):
+    repo=Path(repo) if repo is not None else repo_root()
+    if legacy_read_only:
+        try: schema=_load_json(ct_root(repo)/'schemas/execution_binding.schema.json')
+        except PackageBlocked as exc: return [str(exc)]
+        errors=schema_errors(binding,schema)
+    else:
+        errors=governed_schema_errors(binding,'execution_binding.v2',repo)
+    if errors: return errors
+    if not strict_identifier(binding.get('packet_id',binding.get('macro_id'))): errors.append('invalid binding subject identity')
+    if not legacy_read_only and not strict_identifier(binding['macro_id']): errors.append('invalid macro identity')
+    audit=binding['audit']; lane=binding['delivery']['risk_lane']
+    upstream=[entry['source_ref'] for entry in audit['upstream_obligations'] if entry['required']]
+    if (lane in {'L2','L3'} or upstream) and not audit['effective_required']:
+        errors.append('upstream/delivery audit obligation cannot be weakened')
+    required_sources=set(upstream)
+    if lane in {'L2','L3'}: required_sources.add(f'delivery:{lane}')
+    if not required_sources.issubset(audit['effective_requirement_sources']):
+        errors.append('audit requirement sources omit upstream/delivery obligation')
+    if audit['effective_required'] and not audit['effective_requirement_sources']:
+        errors.append('effective audit requires requirement sources')
+    policy=binding['policy_refs']
+    if policy['dcp_status']=='PASS' and policy['dcp_route_decision_ref'] in {'UNKNOWN','NOT_RUN'}:
+        errors.append('DCP PASS requires an external policy reference')
+    if policy['dcp_status']=='NOT_RUN' and policy['dcp_risk_class']!='NOT_RUN':
+        errors.append('DCP risk cannot be inferred without DCP policy')
+    constraints=binding['constraints']
+    if set(constraints['allowed_actions']) & set(constraints['forbidden_actions']):
+        errors.append('binding allowed/forbidden action conflict')
+    if set(constraints['allowed_actions']) & {'merge','activate','mark_ready','force_push'}:
+        errors.append('binding cannot create operator gate authority')
+    fallback=binding['fallback']
+    if fallback['allowed'] and (fallback['trigger'] in {'UNKNOWN','NOT_RUN'} or
+            any(value in {'UNKNOWN','NOT_RUN'} for value in fallback['binding'].values())):
+        errors.append('fallback requires explicit trigger and exact pre-authorized route')
+    return errors
+
+
+def cmd_binding_record(args):
+    repo=repo_root(); binding=_load_json(args.file); errors=validate_binding_obj(binding,repo)
+    if errors: raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_BINDING: '+'; '.join(errors))
+    path=binding_path(repo,binding.get('packet_id',binding.get('macro_id'))); _write_json(path,binding); print(path); return 0
+
+
+def cmd_validate_binding(args):
+    errors=validate_binding_obj(_load_json(args.file),legacy_read_only=getattr(args,'legacy_read_only',False))
+    if errors: print('\n'.join('ERROR: '+error for error in errors)); return 2
+    print('PASS: '+('legacy read-only compatibility' if getattr(args,'legacy_read_only',False) else 'GEC v2 ExecutionBinding')+' valid; execution authority NONE'); return 0
+
+
+def _relative_surface(value):
+    """Conservative portable overlap domain: literal path or terminal /** only."""
+    if not isinstance(value,str) or not value or '\\' in value: raise ValueError('invalid relative surface')
+    prefix=value.endswith('/**'); raw=value[:-3] if prefix else value.rstrip('/')
+    if not raw or raw.startswith('/') or any(part in {'','.','..'} for part in raw.split('/')):
+        raise ValueError('unsafe relative surface')
+    if any(char in raw for char in '*?[]{}'): raise ValueError('unsupported write-surface pattern')
+    return raw.casefold(),prefix or value.endswith('/')
+
+
+def _surface_contains(parent,child):
+    p,recursive=_relative_surface(parent); c,child_recursive=_relative_surface(child)
+    return (p==c and (recursive or not child_recursive)) or (recursive and c.startswith(p+'/'))
+
+
+def _surfaces_overlap(left,right):
+    return any(_surface_contains(a,b) or _surface_contains(b,a) for a in left for b in right)
+
+
+def _bound_reference(root,reference,read_json=False):
+    raw=reference['path']; path,recursive=_relative_surface(raw)
+    if recursive: raise PackageBlocked('immutable reference must name one file')
+    candidate=Path(root)
+    for part in raw.split('/'):
+        candidate=candidate/part
+        if candidate.is_symlink(): raise PackageBlocked('immutable reference cannot traverse symlink')
+    if not candidate.resolve().is_relative_to(Path(root).resolve()): raise PackageBlocked('reference escaped package')
+    if not candidate.is_file(): raise PackageBlocked('immutable reference missing')
+    digest,_size=scan_regular_file(candidate)
+    if digest!=reference['sha256']: raise PackageBlocked('immutable reference digest mismatch')
+    if read_json:
+        descriptor=os.open(candidate,os.O_RDONLY|getattr(os,'O_NOFOLLOW',0)|getattr(os,'O_NONBLOCK',0))
+        with os.fdopen(descriptor,'rb') as source:
+            if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(source.fileno()).st_mode): raise PackageBlocked('immutable reference is not regular')
+            data=source.read()
+        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=reference['sha256']:
+            raise PackageBlocked('immutable JSON reference changed before parsing')
+        return _json_data(data,str(candidate))
+    return candidate
+
+
+def macro_pairs(macro):
+    children={child['workstream_id']:child for child in macro['workstreams']}
+    def ancestors(key):
+        found=set(); pending=list(children[key]['dependencies'])
+        while pending:
+            current=pending.pop()
+            if current not in found:
+                found.add(current); pending.extend(children[current]['dependencies'])
+        return found
+    pairs=[]
+    for a,b in combinations(sorted(children),2):
+        left=children[a]; right=children[b]
+        if a in ancestors(b) or b in ancestors(a): classification='DEPENDENCY_SERIALIZED'
+        elif not (left['mutating'] and right['mutating']): classification='READ_ONLY_COMPANION'
+        elif _surfaces_overlap(left['write_surface'],right['write_surface']): classification='WRITE_OVERLAP'
+        elif set(left['canonical_writers']) & set(right['canonical_writers']): classification='SHARED_CANONICAL_WRITER'
+        elif set(left['semantic_scope']) & set(right['semantic_scope']): classification='SEMANTIC_OVERLAP'
+        else: classification='DISJOINT'
+        pairs.append({'workstreams':[a,b],'classification':classification})
+    return pairs
+
+
+def validate_macro_obj(macro,repo=None,package_root=None,*,legacy_read_only=False):
+    repo=Path(repo) if repo is not None else repo_root()
+    if legacy_read_only:
+        try: schema=_load_json(ct_root(repo)/'schemas/supervisor_macro_packet.schema.json')
+        except PackageBlocked as exc: return [str(exc)]
+        errors=schema_errors(macro,schema)
+    else:
+        errors=governed_schema_errors(macro,'macro_packet.v2',repo)
+    if errors: return errors
+    ids=[child['workstream_id'] for child in macro['workstreams']]
+    if len(set(ids))!=len(ids) or not all(strict_identifier(key) for key in ids): errors.append('duplicate/invalid child identity')
+    if not strict_identifier(macro['macro_id']): errors.append('invalid macro identity')
+    if macro['parallelism']['max_mutating_workstreams']<0: errors.append('negative parallel limit')
+    graph={child['workstream_id']:child['dependencies'] for child in macro['workstreams']}
+    remaining=set(graph)
+    while remaining:
+        ready={key for key in remaining if not (set(graph[key]) & remaining)}
+        if not ready: errors.append('dependency DAG contains a cycle'); break
+        remaining-=ready
+    for child in macro['workstreams']:
+        if any(dep not in graph for dep in child['dependencies']): errors.append('dependency does not resolve')
+        if len(set(child['dependencies']))!=len(child['dependencies']): errors.append('duplicate dependency')
+        try:
+            for surface in child['write_surface']: _relative_surface(surface)
+        except ValueError as exc: errors.append(str(exc))
+        if child['mutating'] and any(not child[key] for key in ('write_surface','canonical_writers','semantic_scope')):
+            errors.append('mutating child requires write, semantic and canonical-writer bounds')
+        if not child['mutating'] and child['write_surface']: errors.append('read-only child cannot claim writes')
+        if set(child['allowed_actions']) & (set(child['forbidden_actions'])|set(macro['authority']['global_forbidden_actions'])):
+            errors.append('child allowed action violates a denial')
+        if not set(child['allowed_actions']).issubset(macro['authority']['global_allowed_actions']):
+            errors.append('child actions exceed global ceiling')
+        if child['delivery_risk_lane'] in {'L2','L3'} and not child['audit']['required']:
+            errors.append('child delivery audit obligation cannot be waived')
+    join_ids=[join['join_id'] for join in macro['joins']]
+    if len(set(join_ids))!=len(join_ids): errors.append('duplicate join identity')
+    for join in macro['joins']:
+        if not join['requires'] or any(key not in graph for key in join['requires']): errors.append('join dependency does not resolve')
+    if not errors:
+        for pair in macro_pairs(macro):
+            if pair['classification'] in {'WRITE_OVERLAP','SHARED_CANONICAL_WRITER','SEMANTIC_OVERLAP'}:
+                errors.append('parallel conflict: '+'/'.join(pair['workstreams'])+' '+pair['classification'])
+    if package_root is not None:
+        for reference in [macro['team_lead']['execution_binding_ref']]+[child['task_packet_ref'] for child in macro['workstreams']]:
+            try: _bound_reference(package_root,reference)
+            except (PackageBlocked,OSError,ValueError) as exc: errors.append(str(exc))
+        if not errors:
+            try:
+                coordinator=_bound_reference(package_root,macro['team_lead']['execution_binding_ref'],read_json=True)
+                errors.extend(validate_binding_obj(coordinator,repo,legacy_read_only=legacy_read_only))
+                if coordinator.get('macro_id')!=macro['macro_id'] or 'packet_id' in coordinator: errors.append('coordinator binding subject mismatch')
+            except (PackageBlocked,OSError,ValueError) as exc: errors.append(str(exc))
+    return errors
+
+
+def delegation_errors(child,canonical):
+    """Check a proposal against one externally verified child view, never its siblings."""
+    errors=[]
+    if canonical.get('task_packet_sha256')!=child['task_packet_ref']['sha256']:
+        return ['CHILD_SUBJECT_MISMATCH']
+    for key in ('delivery_risk_lane','rollback_boundary','mutating'):
+        if child[key]!=canonical.get(key): errors.append('CHILD_BOUNDARY_CHANGED:'+key)
+    for key in ('canonical_writers','semantic_scope'):
+        if set(child[key])!=set(canonical.get(key,[])): errors.append('CHILD_BOUNDARY_CHANGED:'+key)
+    for key in ('allowed_actions',):
+        if not set(child[key]).issubset(canonical.get(key,[])): errors.append('CHILD_SCOPE_WIDENED:'+key)
+    if not set(canonical.get('forbidden_actions',[])).issubset(child['forbidden_actions']):
+        errors.append('CHILD_DENIAL_REMOVED')
+    try:
+        if any(not any(_surface_contains(bound,path) for bound in canonical.get('write_surface',[])) for path in child['write_surface']):
+            errors.append('CHILD_WRITE_ALLOWLIST_WIDENED')
+    except ValueError: errors.append('CHILD_WRITE_SCOPE_UNKNOWN')
+    required=canonical.get('audit_required')
+    if type(required) is not bool: errors.append('CHILD_AUDIT_OBLIGATION_UNKNOWN')
+    elif required and not child['audit']['required']: errors.append('CHILD_AUDIT_WAIVED')
+    if child['execution_route']!=canonical.get('execution_route'): errors.append('ROUTE_CEILING_EXCEEDED')
+    if set(child['allowed_actions']) & {'merge','activate','mark_ready','force_push'}:
+        errors.append('OPERATOR_GATE_NOT_DELEGABLE')
+    return errors
+
+
+def preview_macro(macro,snapshots,repo=None):
+    """Read-only projection. Project adapters, not model reports, verify upstream inputs."""
+    errors=validate_macro_obj(macro,repo)
+    result={'authority':'NONE','status':'BLOCKED' if errors else 'PREVIEW_ONLY','errors':errors,
+        'children':{},'candidate_batches':[],'pairs':[],'operator_gates_needed':[],
+        'supervisor_decisions_needed':[]}
+    if errors: return result
+    result['pairs']=macro_pairs(macro)
+    result['dcp_summary']={'authority':'NONE','max_delivery_risk_lane':max(
+        (child['delivery_risk_lane'] for child in macro['workstreams']),key=lambda lane:int(lane[1])),
+        'child_dcp_statuses':{child['workstream_id']:child['dcp_status'] for child in macro['workstreams']}}
+    global_stop=any(snapshot.get('global_stop_triggered') is True for snapshot in snapshots.values())
+    candidates=[]
+    for child in sorted(macro['workstreams'],key=lambda value:value['workstream_id']):
+        key=child['workstream_id']; snapshot=snapshots.get(key,{})
+        blockers=[]; canonical=snapshot.get('canonical_authority',{})
+        if snapshot.get('verified_upstream') is not True: blockers.append('UPSTREAM_UNVERIFIED')
+        else: blockers.extend(delegation_errors(child,canonical))
+        if snapshot.get('custody_verified') is not True: blockers.append('CUSTODY_UNVERIFIED')
+        for source in ('operator','repository'):
+            ceiling=snapshot.get(source,{})
+            if not ceiling.get('source_ref') or not set(child['allowed_actions']).issubset(ceiling.get('allowed_actions',[])):
+                blockers.append(source.upper()+'_ACTION_CEILING')
+            if set(child['allowed_actions']) & set(ceiling.get('forbidden_actions',[])):
+                blockers.append(source.upper()+'_DENIAL')
+            if ceiling.get('audit_required') is True and not child['audit']['required']:
+                blockers.append('UPSTREAM_AUDIT_REQUIRED')
+        if global_stop or snapshot.get('global_stop_triggered') is True: blockers.append('GLOBAL_STOP')
+        workflow=snapshot.get('workflow',{})
+        if workflow.get('status')!='LEGAL' or workflow.get('blockers') or not workflow.get('source_ref'):
+            blockers.append('WORKFLOW_NOT_LEGAL')
+        policy=snapshot.get('policy',{})
+        if policy.get('required') is True:
+            if policy.get('status')!='PASS' or not policy.get('source_ref') or child['dcp_status']!='PASS': blockers.append('DCP_NOT_ELIGIBLE')
+        elif policy.get('required') is not False or policy.get('status')!='NOT_APPLICABLE': blockers.append('POLICY_UNKNOWN')
+        elif child['dcp_status']!='NOT_RUN': blockers.append('POLICY_APPLICABILITY_CONFLICT')
+        if child['dcp_status'] in {'UNKNOWN','BLOCKED'}: blockers.append('DCP_NOT_ELIGIBLE')
+        for ceiling in (workflow,policy):
+            if set(child['allowed_actions']) & set(ceiling.get('forbidden_actions',[])):
+                blockers.append('UPSTREAM_DENIAL')
+            if ceiling.get('audit_required') is True and not child['audit']['required']:
+                blockers.append('UPSTREAM_AUDIT_REQUIRED')
+        if not set(child['allowed_actions']).issubset(workflow.get('allowed_actions',[])):
+            blockers.append('WORKFLOW_ACTION_CEILING')
+        if policy.get('required') and not set(child['allowed_actions']).issubset(policy.get('allowed_actions',[])):
+            blockers.append('POLICY_ACTION_CEILING')
+        if child['delivery_risk_lane']=='L3' and snapshot.get('operator_gate_verified') is not True:
+            blockers.append('OPERATOR_GATE_MISSING'); result['operator_gates_needed'].append(key)
+        for dep in child['dependencies']:
+            previous=snapshots.get(dep,{})
+            bound=next(item for item in macro['workstreams'] if item['workstream_id']==dep)
+            if previous.get('status')!='PASS' or previous.get('completion_verified') is not True or previous.get('verified_upstream') is not True or not previous.get('completion_source_ref') or previous.get('canonical_authority',{}).get('task_packet_sha256')!=bound['task_packet_ref']['sha256']:
+                blockers.append('DEPENDENCY_NOT_SATISFIED:'+dep)
+        if snapshot.get('route_failed') is True:
+            fallback=snapshot.get('selected_fallback')
+            if fallback not in child['execution_route']['allowed_fallbacks']: blockers.append('ROUTE_CEILING_EXCEEDED')
+        state=snapshot.get('status','NOT_RUN')
+        if state not in {'NOT_RUN','READY','PASS','FAIL','BLOCKED','UNKNOWN'}: blockers.append('CHILD_STATUS_UNKNOWN')
+        if state in {'FAIL','BLOCKED','UNKNOWN'}: blockers.append('CHILD_'+state)
+        if state=='PASS' and (snapshot.get('completion_verified') is not True or not snapshot.get('completion_source_ref')): blockers.append('COMPLETION_UNVERIFIED')
+        if child['mutating'] and snapshot.get('write_scope_verified') is not True: blockers.append('PHYSICAL_WRITE_SCOPE_UNVERIFIED')
+        result['children'][key]={'reported_status':state,'blockers':sorted(set(blockers)),
+            'candidate':not blockers and state in {'NOT_RUN','READY'},'task_packet_sha256':child['task_packet_ref']['sha256']}
+        if result['children'][key]['candidate']: candidates.append(child)
+    limit=macro['parallelism']['max_mutating_workstreams']; batches=[]
+    for child in candidates:
+        if child['mutating'] and limit==0:
+            result['children'][child['workstream_id']]['candidate']=False
+            result['children'][child['workstream_id']]['blockers'].append('MUTATION_LIMIT_ZERO'); continue
+        if not batches or batches[-1]['group']!=child['parallel_group'] or (child['mutating'] and batches[-1]['mutations']>=limit):
+            batches.append({'group':child['parallel_group'],'mutations':0,'workstreams':[]})
+        batches[-1]['workstreams'].append(child['workstream_id']); batches[-1]['mutations']+=int(child['mutating'])
+    result['candidate_batches']=[batch['workstreams'] for batch in batches]
+    return result
+
+
+def macro_return(macro,child_returns):
+    """Keep model/child reports inert; never aggregate a finality verdict."""
+    children={child['workstream_id']:child for child in macro['workstreams']}; errors=[]
+    if set(children)!=set(child_returns): errors.append('CHILD_RETURN_COVERAGE_MISMATCH')
+    for key,report in child_returns.items():
+        if key not in children or not isinstance(report,dict): errors.append('UNKNOWN_CHILD_RETURN'); continue
+        if report.get('task_packet_sha256')!=children[key]['task_packet_ref']['sha256']: errors.append('CHILD_RETURN_SUBJECT_MISMATCH:'+key)
+        for kind in ('proof','audit'):
+            receipt=report.get(kind)
+            if receipt and (not isinstance(receipt,dict) or not isinstance(report.get('execution_subject'),str) or not report['execution_subject'] or receipt.get('subject')!=report.get('execution_subject') or
+                    receipt.get('task_packet_sha256')!=report.get('task_packet_sha256')):
+                errors.append('RECEIPT_SUBJECT_MISMATCH:'+key+':'+kind)
+    return {'macro_id':macro['macro_id'],'authority':'NONE','status':'BLOCKED' if errors else 'RETURNED_FOR_SUPERVISOR',
+        'children':child_returns,'errors':errors,'operator_gates_needed':sorted({gate for report in child_returns.values() if isinstance(report,dict) for gate in report.get('operator_gates_needed',[])}),
+        'supervisor_decisions_needed':sorted({decision for report in child_returns.values() if isinstance(report,dict) for decision in report.get('supervisor_decisions_needed',[])})}
+
+
+def cmd_validate_macro(args):
+    path=Path(args.file); macro=_load_json(path); errors=validate_macro_obj(macro,package_root=path.parent,legacy_read_only=getattr(args,'legacy_read_only',False))
+    valid_status='LEGACY_READ_ONLY_VALID' if getattr(args,'legacy_read_only',False) else 'STATIC_VALID'
+    print(json.dumps({'status':'FAIL' if errors else valid_status,'authority':'NONE','errors':errors},sort_keys=True))
+    return 2 if errors else 0
+
+def assert_matching_identities(*identities):
+    if not identities or any(not strict_identifier(value) for value in identities):
+        raise PackageBlocked('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: canonical packet id required')
+    if len(set(identities))!=1: raise PackageBlocked('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISMATCH: package identity chain differs')
+    return identities[0]
+
+def _frontmatter_id(lines):
+    if not lines or lines[0].strip()!='---': return None,0
+    end=None
+    for index in range(1,len(lines)):
+        if lines[index].strip()=='---': end=index; break
+    if end is None: raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS: unterminated frontmatter')
+    values=[]
+    for line in lines[1:end]:
+        match=re.match(r'^(?:id|packet_id|task_packet_id)\s*:\s*(.*?)\s*$',line,re.I)
+        if match:
+            value=match.group(1).strip().strip('"\'`')
+            if value: values.append(value)
+    if len(set(values))>1: raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS: conflicting frontmatter ids')
+    return (values[0] if values else None),end+1
+
+def _markdown_packet_identity(path):
+    try: lines=Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
+    except (OSError,UnicodeError) as exc: raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: unreadable Markdown packet') from exc
+    front,start=_frontmatter_id(lines); values=[]
+    if front: values.append(front)
+    for index,line in enumerate(lines[start:],start):
+        if re.match(r'^##\s+Packet metadata\s*$',line,re.I):
+            cursor=index+1
+            while cursor<len(lines) and not re.match(r'^\s*(`{3,}|~{3,})',lines[cursor]): cursor+=1
+            if cursor>=len(lines): continue
+            opener=re.match(r'^\s*(`{3,}|~{3,})',lines[cursor]).group(1); cursor+=1
+            while cursor<len(lines) and not re.match(r'^\s*'+re.escape(opener[0])+r'{'+str(len(opener))+r',}',lines[cursor]):
+                match=re.match(r'^(?:packet_id|task_packet_id)\s*:\s*`?([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{2,127})`?\s*$',lines[cursor],re.I)
+                if match: values.append(match.group(1))
+                cursor+=1
+    fence=None
+    heading=re.compile(r'^#\s+Task Packet\s*(?::|\u2014|-)\s*`?([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{2,127})`?(?:\s|$)')
+    for line in lines[start:]:
+        marker=re.match(r'^\s*(`{3,}|~{3,})',line)
+        if marker:
+            token=marker.group(1)
+            if fence is None: fence=(token[0],len(token))
+            elif token[0]==fence[0] and len(token)>=fence[1]: fence=None
+            continue
+        if fence is not None: continue
+        match=heading.match(line)
+        if match: values.append(match.group(1))
+    unique=set(values)
+    if not unique: raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: no authoritative packet id')
+    if len(unique)!=1: raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS: conflicting authoritative packet ids')
+    identity=next(iter(unique))
+    if not strict_identifier(identity): raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: invalid packet id')
+    return identity
+
+def packet_identity(path):
+    path=Path(path)
+    if path.suffix.lower()=='.json':
+        value=_load_json(path)
+        if not isinstance(value,dict) or not strict_identifier(value.get('id')):
+            raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: JSON packet requires canonical id')
+        return value['id']
+    return _markdown_packet_identity(path)
+
+def proof_identity(path):
+    value=_load_json(path)
+    if not isinstance(value,dict): raise PacketIdentityError('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: canonical proof must be an object')
+    found=[value[key] for key in ('packet_id','tp_id') if key in value]
+    return assert_matching_identities(*found)
+
+def git_capture(repo,*args):
+    result=run(['git','-c','core.fsmonitor=false',*args],repo)
+    if result['returncode']!=0: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_GIT_EVIDENCE: git {" ".join(args)} failed')
+    return result['stdout']
+
+def git_remote_url(repo):
+    try: return git_capture(repo,'remote','get-url','origin').strip()
+    except PackageBlocked: return None
+
+def _normalize_origin(value):
+    if not isinstance(value,str) or not value.strip() or any(x in value for x in ('?','#','\\')): return None
+    raw=value.strip(); host='github.com'; path=''
+    if re.match(r'^[^/@\s]+@[^/:\s]+:',raw):
+        userhost,path=raw.split(':',1); host=userhost.split('@',1)[1].lower()
+    elif '://' in raw:
+        try: parsed=urlsplit(raw)
+        except ValueError: return None
+        if parsed.query or parsed.fragment or parsed.username not in (None,'git') or parsed.password: return None
+        if parsed.scheme not in ('http','https','ssh') or not parsed.hostname: return None
+        host=parsed.hostname.lower()
+        port=parsed.port
+        if port and not ((parsed.scheme=='https' and port==443) or (parsed.scheme=='http' and port==80) or (parsed.scheme=='ssh' and port==22)): host=f'{host}:{port}'
+        path=parsed.path
+    else: path=raw
+    path=path.strip('/')
+    if path.endswith('.git'): path=path[:-4]
+    parts=path.split('/')
+    if len(parts)!=2 or not all(re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+',part) for part in parts): return None
+    return (host+'/'+path).lower()
+
+def _safe_marker(repo,marker):
+    if not isinstance(marker,str) or not marker or '\\' in marker: return None
+    pure=PurePosixPath(marker)
+    if pure.is_absolute() or '..' in pure.parts: return None
+    path=repo/marker
+    try:
+        current=repo
+        for part in pure.parts:
+            current=current/part
+            info=current.lstat()
+            if stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode): return None
+        if not path.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(repo.resolve(strict=True)): return None
+    except OSError: return None
+    return path
+
+def validate_packet_binding(repo,packet):
+    packet=Path(packet); repo=Path(repo)
+    if packet.suffix.lower()!='.json': return
+    value=_load_json(packet)
+    if not isinstance(value,dict): raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_REPO_IDENTITY_MISMATCH: packet is not an object')
+    if 'repo_binding' not in value: return
+    binding=value.get('repo_binding')
+    if not isinstance(binding,dict) or type(binding.get('require_identity_match')) is not bool:
+        raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_REPO_IDENTITY_MISMATCH: malformed repository binding')
+    if binding.get('require_identity_match') is not True: return
+    project=load_project(repo).get('project_name'); expected=binding.get('project_id')
+    remote=_normalize_origin(git_remote_url(repo)); hinted=_normalize_origin(binding.get('origin_hint'))
+    if not isinstance(project,str) or expected!=project or _safe_marker(repo,binding.get('repo_marker')) is None or remote is None or hinted is None or remote!=hinted:
+        raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_REPO_IDENTITY_MISMATCH: project, marker, or origin does not match installed repository')
+
+def validate_return_metadata(metadata,repo=None):
+    repo=Path(repo) if repo is not None else repo_root()
+    try: schema=_load_json(ct_root(repo)/'schemas/return_packet.schema.json')
+    except PackageBlocked as exc: return [str(exc)]
+    return schema_errors(metadata,schema)
+
+def build_return_metadata(packet_id,stamp,project,reason,decision_needed,state,route):
+    if not isinstance(reason,str) or not reason.strip(): raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_RETURN_METADATA: reason must be nonblank')
+    if not isinstance(decision_needed,str) or not decision_needed.strip(): raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_RETURN_METADATA: decision_needed must be nonblank')
+    metadata={'return_id':f'RETURN-{project}-{packet_id}-{stamp}','packet_id':packet_id,'reason':reason.strip(),'decision_needed':decision_needed.strip(),'head_sha':state.get('head_sha'),'base_sha':state.get('merge_base_origin_main'),'branch':state.get('branch'),'project':project,'risk_lane':route.get('delivery',{}).get('risk_lane',route.get('risk_lane'))}
+    return metadata
+
+SECRET_PATTERNS=[
+ ('PRIVATE_KEY', re.compile(rb'-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |)PRIVATE KEY-----')),
+ ('GITHUB_TOKEN', re.compile(rb'\b(?:ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,})\b')),
+ ('AWS_ACCESS_KEY', re.compile(rb'\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b')),
+]
+_VALUE_BYTES=frozenset(b'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_./+-=')
+_WORD_BYTES=frozenset(b'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_')
+_EXAMPLE_PREFIXES=(b'<',b'${',b'redacted',b'changeme',b'example',b'none',b'null')
+_ASSIGNMENT_KEY_PATTERN=re.compile(rb'(?i)(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])(?:password|passwd|api[_-]?key|token|secret)(?=\s|[:=])')
+
+class StreamingSecretScanner:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.carry=b''; self.state='search'; self.search_tail=bytearray(); self.value=bytearray()
+        self.openai_state=0; self.openai_count=0; self.openai_prev_word=False
+    def _reset(self): self.state='search'; self.search_tail.clear(); self.value.clear()
+    def _assignment_data(self,chunk):
+        data=(bytes(self.search_tail)+chunk) if self.state=='search' else chunk
+        self.search_tail.clear(); index=0
+        while index<len(data):
+            if self.state=='search':
+                match=_ASSIGNMENT_KEY_PATTERN.search(data,index)
+                if match is None:
+                    self.search_tail.extend(data[-32:]); return
+                self.state='after_key'; index=match.end(); continue
+            byte=data[index]; whitespace=bytes((byte,)).isspace()
+            if self.state=='after_key':
+                if whitespace: index+=1; continue
+                if byte in (58,61): self.state='after_separator'; index+=1; continue
+                self._reset(); continue
+            if self.state=='after_separator':
+                if whitespace: index+=1; continue
+                if byte in (34,39): self.state='value'; index+=1; continue
+                if byte in _VALUE_BYTES: self.state='value'; continue
+                self._reset(); continue
+            if byte in _VALUE_BYTES:
+                if len(self.value)<32: self.value.append(byte+32 if 65<=byte<=90 else byte)
+                if bytes(self.value) in _EXAMPLE_PREFIXES:
+                    self._reset(); index+=1; continue
+                if len(self.value)>=16 and not any(bytes(self.value).startswith(prefix) for prefix in _EXAMPLE_PREFIXES):
+                    raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: SECRET_ASSIGNMENT')
+                index+=1; continue
+            self._reset()
+    def _openai_data(self,chunk):
+        for byte in chunk:
+            if self.openai_state==0:
+                if byte==115 and not self.openai_prev_word: self.openai_state=1
+                self.openai_prev_word=byte in _WORD_BYTES
+                continue
+            if self.openai_state==1:
+                self.openai_state=2 if byte==107 else 0
+                self.openai_prev_word=byte in _WORD_BYTES
+                continue
+            if self.openai_state==2:
+                self.openai_state=3 if byte==45 else 0
+                self.openai_count=0
+                self.openai_prev_word=byte in _WORD_BYTES
+                continue
+            if byte in _VALUE_BYTES:
+                self.openai_count+=1
+                self.openai_prev_word=byte in _WORD_BYTES
+                if self.openai_count>=20 and self.openai_prev_word:
+                    raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: OPENAI_KEY')
+                continue
+            boundary=not self.openai_prev_word
+            self.openai_state=1 if byte==115 and boundary else 0
+            self.openai_count=0
+            self.openai_prev_word=byte in _WORD_BYTES
+    def feed(self,chunk):
+        data=self.carry+chunk
+        for name,pattern in SECRET_PATTERNS:
+            if pattern.search(data): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: {name}')
+        self.carry=data[-128:]
+        self._openai_data(chunk)
+        self._assignment_data(chunk)
+
+def _regular_stat(path):
+    try: info=Path(path).lstat()
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: unreadable file {path}') from exc
+    if stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode) or not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: regular non-symlink file required: {path}')
+    return info
+
+def scan_regular_file(path):
+    path=Path(path); before=_regular_stat(path); scanner=StreamingSecretScanner(); digest=hashlib.sha256(); count=0
+    try:
+        descriptor=os.open(path,os.O_RDONLY|getattr(os,'O_NOFOLLOW',0))
+        try:
+            opened=os.fstat(descriptor)
+            if (opened.st_dev,opened.st_ino)!=(before.st_dev,before.st_ino): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: {path}')
+            while True:
+                chunk=os.read(descriptor,CHUNK_SIZE)
+                if not chunk: break
+                scanner.feed(chunk); digest.update(chunk); count+=len(chunk)
+            after=os.fstat(descriptor)
+        finally: os.close(descriptor)
+    except PackageBlocked: raise
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: unreadable file {path}') from exc
+    if count!=before.st_size: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_SECRET_SCAN: partial read for {path}; expected {before.st_size}, got {count}')
+    if (after.st_dev,after.st_ino,after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns)!=(before.st_dev,before.st_ino,before.st_size,before.st_mtime_ns):
+        raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: file changed while scanning: {path}')
+    return digest.hexdigest(),count
+
+def scan_staged_tree(root):
+    root=Path(root); digests={}; counters={'files_scanned':0,'bytes_scanned':0,'files_skipped':0,'files_unreadable':0,'scan_errors':0}
+    def walk_error(exc): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: traversal failed: {exc}')
+    try: walked=list(os.walk(root,topdown=True,onerror=walk_error,followlinks=False))
+    except PackageBlocked: raise
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: traversal failed: {exc}') from exc
+    for directory,dirnames,filenames in walked:
+        base=Path(directory)
+        for name in dirnames:
+            if (base/name).is_symlink(): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: symlink in staged package: {base/name}')
+        for name in sorted(filenames):
+            path=base/name; digest,size=scan_regular_file(path)
+            digests[path.relative_to(root).as_posix()]=(digest,size)
+            counters['files_scanned']+=1; counters['bytes_scanned']+=size
+    return digests,counters
+
+def scan_files(paths):
+    hits=[]
+    for path in paths:
+        try: scan_regular_file(path)
+        except PackageBlocked as exc: hits.append({'path':str(path),'pattern':str(exc).split(': ')[-1]})
+    return hits
+
+def _write_json(path,value): Path(path).write_text(json.dumps(value,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n')
+
+def _local_git_state(repo):
+    return {
+        'repo_root':str(repo),
+        'head_sha':git_capture(repo,'rev-parse','HEAD').strip(),
+        'branch':git_capture(repo,'branch','--show-current').strip(),
+        'dirty':bool(git_capture(repo,'status','--porcelain=v1').strip()),
+        'status_porcelain':git_capture(repo,'status','--porcelain=v1'),
+        'origin':git_capture(repo,'remote','get-url','origin').strip(),
+        'merge_base_origin_main':git_capture(repo,'merge-base','HEAD','origin/main').strip(),
+        'captured_at':now_stamp(),
+    }
+
+def _copy_regular(source,destination):
+    source=Path(source); destination=Path(destination); before=_regular_stat(source); destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+    src=None; dst=None; count=0
+    try:
+        src=os.open(source,os.O_RDONLY|getattr(os,'O_NOFOLLOW',0)); opened=os.fstat(src)
+        if (opened.st_dev,opened.st_ino)!=(before.st_dev,before.st_ino): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: {source}')
+        dst=os.open(destination,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL,0o600)
+        while True:
+            chunk=os.read(src,CHUNK_SIZE)
+            if not chunk: break
+            view=memoryview(chunk)
+            while view:
+                written=os.write(dst,view)
+                if written<=0: raise OSError('partial destination write')
+                view=view[written:]
+            count+=len(chunk)
+        after=os.fstat(src)
+    except PackageBlocked: raise
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_COPY: {source}') from exc
+    finally:
+        if src is not None: os.close(src)
+        if dst is not None: os.close(dst)
+    if count!=before.st_size or (after.st_dev,after.st_ino,after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns)!=(before.st_dev,before.st_ino,before.st_size,before.st_mtime_ns):
+        raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: partial or changed source {source}')
+
+def _copy_input(source,destination):
+    source=Path(source); destination=Path(destination)
+    try: info=source.lstat()
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_COPY: missing input {source}') from exc
+    if stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: symlink input {source}')
+    if stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode): _copy_regular(source,destination); return
+    if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: special input {source}')
+    destination.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
+    def walk_error(exc): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: input traversal failed: {exc}')
+    try: walked=list(os.walk(source,topdown=True,onerror=walk_error,followlinks=False))
+    except OSError as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: input traversal failed: {exc}') from exc
+    for directory,dirnames,filenames in walked:
+        base=Path(directory); relative=base.relative_to(source); target=destination/relative
+        for name in dirnames:
+            child=base/name
+            if child.is_symlink(): raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_UNSAFE_PATH: symlink input {child}')
+            (target/name).mkdir(exist_ok=False)
+        for name in filenames: _copy_regular(base/name,target/name)
+
+def _validate_staged_identities(root,packet_id,kind,repo):
+    tasks=[path for path in root.iterdir() if path.is_file() and path.name.startswith('TASK_PACKET.')]
+    if len(tasks)!=1: raise PackageBlocked('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_MISSING: one staged Task Packet required')
+    validate_packet_binding(repo,tasks[0]); identities=[packet_id,packet_identity(tasks[0])]
+    route=_load_json(root/'EXECUTION_BINDING.json'); route_errors=validate_binding_obj(route,repo)
+    if route_errors: raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_ROUTE_INVALID: '+'; '.join(route_errors))
+    identities.append(route.get('packet_id',route.get('macro_id')) if isinstance(route,dict) else None)
+    proof=root/'PROOF_REVIEW'/'PROOF.json'
+    if proof.exists(): identities.append(proof_identity(proof))
+    if kind=='RETURN':
+        metadata=_load_json(root/'RETURN_METADATA.json'); identities.append(metadata.get('packet_id') if isinstance(metadata,dict) else None)
+    assert_matching_identities(*identities)
+
+def _write_manifests(root,primary,counters):
+    report={'status':'PASS','hits':[],'counter_scope':'primary staged payload','files_scanned':counters['files_scanned'],'bytes_scanned':counters['bytes_scanned'],'files_skipped':0,'files_unreadable':0,'scan_errors':0}
+    report_path=root/'SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json'; _write_json(report_path,report); report_digest=scan_regular_file(report_path)
+    inventory_inputs=dict(primary); inventory_inputs['SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json']=report_digest
+    inventory=[{'path':name,'sha256':value[0],'bytes':value[1]} for name,value in sorted(inventory_inputs.items())]
+    inventory_path=root/'FILE_INVENTORY.json'; _write_json(inventory_path,inventory); inventory_digest=scan_regular_file(inventory_path)
+    sums_inputs=dict(inventory_inputs); sums_inputs['FILE_INVENTORY.json']=inventory_digest
+    sums_path=root/'SHA256SUMS.txt'; sums_path.write_text(''.join(f'{value[0]}  {name}\n' for name,value in sorted(sums_inputs.items())))
+    sums_digest=scan_regular_file(sums_path)
+    expected=dict(sums_inputs); expected['SHA256SUMS.txt']=sums_digest
+    final,final_counters=scan_staged_tree(root)
+    if final!=expected or final_counters['files_scanned']!=len(expected): raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: final package bytes differ from screened bytes')
+    return final
+
+def _write_zip_streamed(root,archive_path,expected):
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path,'w',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
+        for relative,(wanted_digest,wanted_size) in sorted(expected.items()):
+            path=root/relative; digest=hashlib.sha256(); count=0
+            with path.open('rb') as source,archive.open(f'{root.name}/{relative}','w') as destination:
+                while True:
+                    chunk=source.read(CHUNK_SIZE)
+                    if not chunk: break
+                    destination.write(chunk); digest.update(chunk); count+=len(chunk)
+            if digest.hexdigest()!=wanted_digest or count!=wanted_size: raise PackageBlocked(f'BLOCKED_STAGING_DRIFT: ZIP input changed: {relative}')
+
+def _sha256_file(path):
+    digest=hashlib.sha256(); count=0
+    with Path(path).open('rb') as source:
+        while True:
+            chunk=source.read(CHUNK_SIZE)
+            if not chunk: break
+            digest.update(chunk); count+=len(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest(),count
+
+def package(kind,args):
+    try:
+        repo=repo_root(); project_config=load_project(repo); project=project_config.get('project_name')
+        if not strict_identifier(args.packet_id) or not strict_identifier(project): raise PackageBlocked('BLOCK_PACKET_IDENTITY_INVALID: packet or project id')
+        route_path_value=get_route(repo,args.packet_id); route=_load_json(route_path_value)
+        packet=Path(args.packet).absolute(); validate_packet_binding(repo,packet)
+        assert_matching_identities(args.packet_id,route.get('packet_id'),packet_identity(packet))
+        proof=Path(args.proof_dir).absolute() if args.proof_dir else None
+        includes=[Path(value).absolute() for value in args.include]
+        state=_local_git_state(repo); live=state_dir(repo)/'LIVE_STATE.json'; _write_json(live,state)
+        stamp=now_stamp(); package_root=f'{kind}_{project}_{args.packet_id}_{stamp}'
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='ct-pack-') as raw:
+            root=Path(raw)/package_root; root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
+            task_member='TASK_PACKET'+packet.suffix.lower(); _copy_regular(packet,root/task_member)
+            _write_json(root/'EXECUTION_BINDING.json',route); _copy_regular(live,root/'LIVE_STATE.json')
+            (root/'GIT_STATUS.txt').write_text(git_capture(repo,'status','--porcelain=v1','--branch'))
+            (root/'COMMITTED_DIFF.patch').write_text(git_capture(repo,'diff','--binary',state['merge_base_origin_main']+'..'+state['head_sha']))
+            (root/'WORKTREE_DIFF.patch').write_text(git_capture(repo,'diff','--binary','HEAD'))
+            if proof: _copy_input(proof,root/'PROOF_REVIEW')
+            if includes:
+                (root/'INCLUDED').mkdir()
+                for index,source in enumerate(includes,1): _copy_input(source,root/'INCLUDED'/f'{index:02d}_{source.name}')
+            manifest={'schema_version':'1.0','kind':kind,'packet_id':args.packet_id,'project':project,'package_root':package_root,'task_packet_member':task_member,'inventory_scope':'all members except FILE_INVENTORY.json and SHA256SUMS.txt','sha256sums_scope':'all members except SHA256SUMS.txt'}
+            _write_json(root/'MANIFEST.json',manifest)
+            if kind=='RETURN':
+                metadata=build_return_metadata(args.packet_id,stamp,project,args.reason,args.decision_needed,state,route)
+                errors=validate_return_metadata(metadata,repo)
+                if errors: raise PackageBlocked('BLOCKED_RETURN_METADATA: '+'; '.join(errors))
+                _write_json(root/'RETURN_METADATA.json',metadata)
+                template=(ct_root(repo)/'templates/ARCHITECTURE_RETURN_PACKET.template.md').read_text()
+                values={'RETURN_ID':metadata['return_id'],'PACKET_ID':args.packet_id,'PROJECT':project,'REPOSITORY':state['origin'],'BASE_SHA':state['merge_base_origin_main'],'HEAD_SHA':state['head_sha'],'BRANCH':state['branch'],'RISK_LANE':route['delivery']['risk_lane'],'REASON':metadata['reason'],'DECISION_NEEDED':metadata['decision_needed']}
+                for key,value in values.items(): template=template.replace('{{'+key+'}}',str(value))
+                (root/'ARCHITECTURE_RETURN_PACKET.md').write_text(template)
+            _validate_staged_identities(root,args.packet_id,kind,repo)
+            primary,counters=scan_staged_tree(root); expected=_write_manifests(root,primary,counters)
+            private=Path(raw)/(package_root+'.zip'); _write_zip_streamed(root,private,expected)
+            code,message=verify_zip_file(private)
+            if code!=0: raise PackageBlocked(message)
+            outdir=expand(project_config['downloads']['proof' if kind=='PROOF' else 'return']); outdir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+            output=outdir/f'{stamp}_{project}_{args.packet_id}_{kind}.zip'; temporary_output=None
+            try:
+                with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=outdir,prefix='.ct-publish-',suffix='.tmp',delete=False) as destination,private.open('rb') as source:
+                    temporary_output=Path(destination.name); shutil.copyfileobj(source,destination,CHUNK_SIZE); destination.flush(); os.fsync(destination.fileno())
+                code,message=verify_zip_file(temporary_output)
+                if code!=0: raise PackageBlocked(message)
+                digest,_size=_sha256_file(temporary_output)
+                os.link(temporary_output,output)
+            finally:
+                if temporary_output is not None: temporary_output.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+            print(output); print('SHA256='+digest)
+        return 0
+    except (PackageBlocked,OSError,KeyError) as exc:
+        print(json.dumps({'status':'BLOCKED_PACKAGE','reason':str(exc)},indent=2),file=sys.stderr); return 3
+
+def cmd_proof_pack(args): return package('PROOF',args)
+def cmd_return_pack(args): return package('RETURN',args)
+
+def _json_data(data,label):
+    def reject_duplicates(pairs):
+        value={}
+        for key,item in pairs:
+            if key in value: raise PackageBlocked(f'duplicate JSON key {key} in {label}')
+            value[key]=item
+        return value
+    try: return json.loads(data.decode('utf-8'),object_pairs_hook=reject_duplicates)
+    except PackageBlocked: raise
+    except Exception as exc: raise PackageBlocked(f'invalid JSON in {label}: {type(exc).__name__}') from exc
+
+def _safe_zip_member(info):
+    name=info.filename
+    if not name or '\\' in name or '\x00' in name: raise PackageBlocked('unsafe ZIP member path')
+    pure=PurePosixPath(name)
+    raw_parts=name.split('/')
+    if pure.is_absolute() or pure.as_posix()!=name or any(part in ('','.', '..') for part in raw_parts): raise PackageBlocked('unsafe ZIP member path')
+    mode=(info.external_attr>>16)&0o170000
+    if mode==stat.S_IFLNK: raise PackageBlocked('symlink ZIP member is forbidden')
+    if mode not in (0,stat.S_IFREG): raise PackageBlocked('special ZIP member is forbidden')
+    if info.is_dir(): raise PackageBlocked('directory ZIP entries are forbidden')
+    return pure
+
+def _parse_inventory(data):
+    value=_json_data(data,'FILE_INVENTORY.json')
+    if not isinstance(value,list): raise PackageBlocked('FILE_INVENTORY must be an array')
+    result={}
+    for item in value:
+        if not isinstance(item,dict) or set(item)!={'path','sha256','bytes'}: raise PackageBlocked('invalid FILE_INVENTORY entry')
+        path=item['path']; digest=item['sha256']; size=item['bytes']
+        if path in result: raise PackageBlocked('duplicate FILE_INVENTORY path')
+        if not isinstance(path,str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}',digest or '') or type(size) is not int or size<0: raise PackageBlocked('invalid FILE_INVENTORY entry')
+        result[path]=(digest,size)
+    return result
+
+def _parse_sums(data):
+    try: lines=data.decode('utf-8').splitlines()
+    except UnicodeError as exc: raise PackageBlocked('invalid SHA256SUMS encoding') from exc
+    result={}
+    for line in lines:
+        match=re.fullmatch(r'([0-9a-f]{64})  ([^\\]+)',line)
+        if not match or match.group(2) in result: raise PackageBlocked('invalid or duplicate SHA256SUMS entry')
+        result[match.group(2)]=match.group(1)
+    return result
+
+def verify_zip_file(path,crc_only=False):
+    try:
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
+            infos=archive.infolist()
+            if not infos: raise PackageBlocked('empty ZIP')
+            if len({info.filename for info in infos})!=len(infos): raise PackageBlocked('duplicate ZIP member')
+            pure_names=[_safe_zip_member(info) for info in infos]
+            roots={name.parts[0] for name in pure_names}
+            if len(roots)!=1 or any(len(name.parts)<2 for name in pure_names): raise PackageBlocked('ZIP must have exactly one package root')
+            if crc_only:
+                bad=archive.testzip()
+                if bad: raise PackageBlocked(f'corrupt ZIP member {bad}')
+                return 0,f'PASS: CRC-only; manifests not verified; members={len(infos)}'
+            root=next(iter(roots)); calculated={}; blobs={}
+            retained={'EXECUTION_BINDING.json','ROUTING_DECISION.json','MANIFEST.json','SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json','FILE_INVENTORY.json','SHA256SUMS.txt','RETURN_METADATA.json','PROOF_REVIEW/PROOF.json'}
+            for info,pure in zip(infos,pure_names):
+                relative=PurePosixPath(*pure.parts[1:]).as_posix(); digest=hashlib.sha256(); scanner=StreamingSecretScanner(); count=0
+                keep=relative in retained or relative.startswith('TASK_PACKET.'); chunks=[] if keep else None
+                with archive.open(info) as stream:
+                    while True:
+                        chunk=stream.read(CHUNK_SIZE)
+                        if not chunk: break
+                        scanner.feed(chunk); digest.update(chunk); count+=len(chunk)
+                        if chunks is not None: chunks.append(chunk)
+                if count!=info.file_size: raise PackageBlocked(f'partial ZIP member read: {relative}')
+                calculated[relative]=(digest.hexdigest(),count)
+                if chunks is not None: blobs[relative]=b''.join(chunks)
+            required={'MANIFEST.json','SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json','FILE_INVENTORY.json','SHA256SUMS.txt'}
+            binding_members=set(calculated)&{'EXECUTION_BINDING.json','ROUTING_DECISION.json'}
+            if len(binding_members)!=1: raise PackageBlocked('exactly one execution binding or historical route member required')
+            binding_member=next(iter(binding_members))
+            task_members=[name for name in calculated if name.startswith('TASK_PACKET.')]
+            if len(task_members)!=1 or not required.issubset(calculated): raise PackageBlocked('required root package members missing')
+            inventory=_parse_inventory(blobs['FILE_INVENTORY.json']); sums=_parse_sums(blobs['SHA256SUMS.txt'])
+            inventory_expected=set(calculated)-{'FILE_INVENTORY.json','SHA256SUMS.txt'}
+            sums_expected=set(calculated)-{'SHA256SUMS.txt'}
+            if set(inventory)!=inventory_expected or set(sums)!=sums_expected: raise PackageBlocked('manifest member coverage mismatch')
+            for name in inventory_expected:
+                if inventory[name]!=calculated[name]: raise PackageBlocked(f'inventory hash or size mismatch: {name}')
+            for name in sums_expected:
+                if sums[name]!=calculated[name][0]: raise PackageBlocked(f'checksum mismatch: {name}')
+            report=_json_data(blobs['SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json'],'SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json')
+            if not isinstance(report,dict) or report.get('status')!='PASS': raise PackageBlocked('missing expected secret PASS receipt')
+            for key in ('files_scanned','bytes_scanned','files_skipped','files_unreadable','scan_errors'):
+                if type(report.get(key)) is not int or report[key]<0: raise PackageBlocked('invalid secret scan receipt counters')
+            if any(report.get(key)!=0 for key in ('files_skipped','files_unreadable','scan_errors')):
+                raise PackageBlocked('secret scan receipt records skipped or failed members')
+            primary_names=set(calculated)-{'SECRET_SCAN_REPORT.json','FILE_INVENTORY.json','SHA256SUMS.txt'}
+            if report['files_scanned']!=len(primary_names) or report['bytes_scanned']!=sum(calculated[name][1] for name in primary_names):
+                raise PackageBlocked('secret scan receipt counters do not match primary payload')
+            manifest=_json_data(blobs['MANIFEST.json'],'MANIFEST.json'); route=_json_data(blobs[binding_member],binding_member)
+            task_name=task_members[0]
+            if task_name.endswith('.json'):
+                task=_json_data(blobs[task_name],task_name); task_id=task.get('id') if isinstance(task,dict) else None
+            else:
+                with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=Path(task_name).suffix) as temporary:
+                    temporary.write(blobs[task_name]); temporary.flush(); task_id=packet_identity(temporary.name)
+            identities=[manifest.get('packet_id') if isinstance(manifest,dict) else None,route.get('packet_id',route.get('macro_id')) if isinstance(route,dict) else None,task_id]
+            proof_name='PROOF_REVIEW/PROOF.json'
+            if proof_name in blobs:
+                proof=_json_data(blobs[proof_name],proof_name); proof_ids=[proof[key] for key in ('packet_id','tp_id') if isinstance(proof,dict) and key in proof]
+                identities.append(assert_matching_identities(*proof_ids))
+            if manifest.get('kind')=='RETURN':
+                if 'RETURN_METADATA.json' not in blobs: raise PackageBlocked('RETURN_METADATA.json missing')
+                metadata=_json_data(blobs['RETURN_METADATA.json'],'RETURN_METADATA.json'); identities.append(metadata.get('packet_id') if isinstance(metadata,dict) else None)
+            packet_id=assert_matching_identities(*identities)
+            if not isinstance(manifest,dict) or manifest.get('schema_version')!='1.0' or manifest.get('package_root')!=root or not strict_identifier(manifest.get('project')) or manifest.get('kind') not in ('PROOF','RETURN'):
+                raise PackageBlocked('package root manifest mismatch')
+            if manifest.get('task_packet_member')!=task_name or manifest.get('inventory_scope')!='all members except FILE_INVENTORY.json and SHA256SUMS.txt' or manifest.get('sha256sums_scope')!='all members except SHA256SUMS.txt':
+                raise PackageBlocked('package manifest scope mismatch')
+            expected_prefix=f"{manifest['kind']}_{manifest['project']}_{packet_id}_"
+            if not root.startswith(expected_prefix): raise PackageBlocked('package root identity mismatch')
+        return 0,f'PASS: ZIP CRC, manifests, inventory, screening, and identity valid; members={len(infos)}'
+    except Exception as exc:
+        return 2,f'FAIL: {exc}'
+
+def cmd_verify_zip(args):
+    z=Path(args.zip).resolve(); code,message=verify_zip_file(z,getattr(args,'crc_only',False)); print(message)
+    if code==0: print('SHA256='+hashlib.sha256(z.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
+    return code
+
+def main():
+    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='ct'); sp=ap.add_subparsers(dest='cmd',required=True)
+    for name,fn in [('doctor',cmd_doctor),('snapshot',cmd_snapshot),('runner-inventory',cmd_runner_inventory)]:
+        p=sp.add_parser(name); p.set_defaults(fn=fn)
+        if name=='runner-inventory': p.add_argument('--probe-models',action='store_true',help='explicitly query configured model catalogs')
+    p=sp.add_parser('route-record'); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_route_record)
+    p.add_argument('--packet-id',required=True); p.add_argument('--stage',required=True); p.add_argument('--risk',required=True,choices=['L0','L1','L2','L3']); p.add_argument('--runner',required=True); p.add_argument('--runner-availability',default='PROVEN',choices=['PROVEN','PROPOSED','UNKNOWN']); p.add_argument('--agent-role',default='implementer'); p.add_argument('--custom-agent'); p.add_argument('--model',required=True); p.add_argument('--effort',required=True); p.add_argument('--why-runner',required=True); p.add_argument('--why-model',required=True); p.add_argument('--why-effort',required=True); p.add_argument('--cost-latency-tradeoff'); p.add_argument('--alternative',action='append',default=[],required=True); p.add_argument('--evidence',action='append',default=[]); p.add_argument('--fallback-runner'); p.add_argument('--fallback-model'); p.add_argument('--fallback-trigger'); p.add_argument('--audit-required',action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,default=None); p.add_argument('--audit-runner'); p.add_argument('--audit-model'); p.add_argument('--audit-effort'); p.add_argument('--audit-independence'); p.add_argument('--audit-rationale')
+    p=sp.add_parser('validate-route'); p.add_argument('--file',required=True); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_validate_route)
+    p=sp.add_parser('bind-record'); p.add_argument('--file',required=True); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_binding_record)
+    p=sp.add_parser('validate-binding'); p.add_argument('--file',required=True); p.add_argument('--legacy-read-only',action='store_true'); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_validate_binding)
+    p=sp.add_parser('validate-macro'); p.add_argument('--file',required=True); p.add_argument('--legacy-read-only',action='store_true'); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_validate_macro)
+    for name,fn in [('proof-pack',cmd_proof_pack),('return-pack',cmd_return_pack)]:
+        p=sp.add_parser(name); p.set_defaults(fn=fn); p.add_argument('--packet-id',required=True); p.add_argument('--packet',required=True); p.add_argument('--proof-dir'); p.add_argument('--include',action='append',default=[]); p.add_argument('--reason',required=name=='return-pack'); p.add_argument('--decision-needed',required=name=='return-pack')
+    p=sp.add_parser('verify-zip'); p.add_argument('--zip',required=True); p.add_argument('--crc-only',action='store_true',help='check ZIP CRC only; do not verify manifests'); p.set_defaults(fn=cmd_verify_zip)
+    args=ap.parse_args()
+    try: result=args.fn(args)
+    except (PackageBlocked,OSError,ValueError) as exc:
+        print('ERROR: '+str(exc),file=sys.stderr); result=2
+    sys.exit(result)
+if __name__=='__main__': main()

+# Architecture Return Protocol
+
+A return is required when the supervisor hits a decision outside ordinary packet execution authority.
+
+Default triggers:
+
+1. canonical authority or system-boundary change;
+2. new public trust/API contract;
+3. proof/signer authority or signature-policy change;
+4. strict finalization / acceptance semantics change;
+5. NOT_REQUIRED / SKIPPED semantics change;
+6. new P0/P1 trust bypass or repeated high-severity pattern;
+7. final independent audit `FAIL` or `NEEDS_SUPERVISOR`;
+8. auditor identity or independence cannot be proven;
+9. only disallowed/paid-per-call audit routes remain where policy forbids them;
+10. credentials, tokens, cookies, keys, or auth material exposed;
+11. branch protection, repository permissions, or production authority must change;
+12. required semantic files fall outside packet allowlist and the expansion is not deterministic synchronization/metadata closure;
+13. major architecture synthesis/audit/ratification conflict;
+14. project-specific stop condition says supervisor/advisor disposition is required.
+
+At a trigger:
+
+```bash
+.control-tower/bin/ct return-pack \
+  --packet-id <id> \
+  --packet <packet path> \
+  --proof-dir <proof path if present> \
+  --reason <trigger> \
+  --decision-needed '<exact decision>' \
+  --include <review bundle or relevant evidence>
+```
+
+The supervisor must preserve existing evidence and stop before unapproved semantic expansion.

+# Operator Gates
+
+The generic baseline keeps these operator-only:
+
+- merge;
+- force push;
+- history rewrite;
+- branch protection changes;
+- branch deletion;
+- credential / permission changes;
+- production mutation;
+- migrations;
+- publication / activation;
+- explicit security residual-risk acceptance.
+
+Project governance may require additional gates such as ready-state promotion or external audit export. Configure them in `.control-tower/project.json` and the active Task Packet.

+# Supervisor MacroPacket Contract v2
+
+Current schema: `schemas/governed_execution/macro_packet.v2.schema.json`.
+CT v1 is retained only for explicit `--legacy-read-only` validation.
+
+A MacroPacket is a multi-workstream delegation envelope, never a pooled authority
+token. The supervisor issues it; the team lead coordinates it. Canonical child
+Task Packets remain repository-owned immutable artifacts, not a new nested kit
+packet schema.
+
+## Required envelope
+
+The installed schema requires identity/objective/repository/base policy, global
+constraints, a coordinator ExecutionBinding reference, child workstreams, DAG
+dependencies/joins, parallel limits, evidence budget and aggregate-return rules.
+Each child retains its objective, preferred/fallback route tuples, delivery risk,
+DCP status/class, workflow reference, allowed/forbidden actions, write surfaces,
+canonical writers, semantic scope, rollback boundary and audit/return requirements.
+
+Child packet and coordinator references use `{path, sha256}`: relative regular
+package members, no symlink traversal, exact digest match. Coordinator binding
+must validate and bind this macro_id. Child formats remain opaque to the kit;
+repository adapters own parsing/authentication of canonical child authority.
+Digest equality establishes byte integrity, not authorship or execution authority.
+
+The JSON template contains placeholder paths/digests. It is a structural example;
+it cannot pass package admission until replaced with actual immutable references.
+
+## Read-only validation and previews
+
+`ct validate-macro --file SUPERVISOR_MACRO_PACKET.json` checks schema, reference
+integrity, coordinator binding, child/edge identities, acyclicity, joins and every
+parallel pair. Success is STATIC_VALID with authority NONE, never authorization.
+
+Write overlap accepts literal relative paths and terminal directory prefixes
+(`path/` or `path/**`). Other glob syntax fails closed. Path comparisons
+conservatively fold case. Shared semantic scope or canonical writer blocks
+parallel mutation; a transitive DAG dependency explicitly serializes the pair.
+Read-only companions may coexist subject to external custody/workflow checks.
+
+`preview_macro(macro, snapshots)` is a pure, advisory projection for tests and
+future project adapters. It never invokes a runner, queries a service, writes
+workflow state, advances a transition or accepts a result. It does not authenticate
+snapshot producers or implement a DCP/Task Orchestrator integration.
+
+A project adapter must first validate immutable package references and authenticate
+current upstream snapshots. It supplies one child-specific snapshot, bound by
+task_packet_sha256, containing:
+
+- canonical_authority: allowed/forbidden actions, exact delivery lane, semantic
+  scope/canonical writers, write allowlist, rollback, mutating flag, audit
+  obligation and exact preferred/fallback route set;
+- verified_upstream plus physical write/custody verification from actual sources;
+- applicable operator/repository action ceilings, denials and source references;
+- legal workflow status, blockers, actions and source reference;
+- policy applicability, status, action bounds, denials and audit requirements;
+- actual child status, operator gate verification, route failure/fallback choice;
+- verified completion and its source reference when satisfying a dependency.
+
+These flags are an internal adapter contract, not self-attestable model fields.
+Passing a model-authored dictionary cannot create execution authority. Unverified,
+unknown, blocked or mismatched inputs hold the affected child. Generic projects
+without DCP explicitly supply required=false and NOT_APPLICABLE, retaining
+dcp_status=NOT_RUN; applicable DCP UNKNOWN/BLOCKED never becomes a candidate.
+
+Candidate batches are inert proposals. A current canonical workflow gate must
+still authorize scheduling and execution outside this kit. Claimed PASS requires
+verified, subject-bound completion evidence before satisfying a dependency.
+Independent failures hold dependents, not unrelated legal siblings. An observed
+global stop propagates to every child; listing stop conditions does not trigger them.
+
+The technical, noncanonical derived dcp_summary has authority NONE, keeps child DCP statuses separate and
+computes maximum delivery lane without mapping the risk taxonomies.
+
+## Delegation and audit invariants
+
+A child cannot take another child's actions or write allowlist, omit known
+canonical writers/semantic scope, change its risk/rollback, remove denials, waive
+audit or use a non-authorized fallback. Effective obligations include repository,
+operator, workflow and applicable policy requirements. L3 holds at its operator
+gate while legal L0/L1 siblings may continue.
+
+The coordinator cannot create canonical packets across authority/risk/writer/
+rollback/audit/security/activation boundaries, change operator-only gates,
+grant merge/activation, or independently audit its own supervised implementation.
+An actual collision, unknown canonical truth, authority conflict, required audit
+failure or route ceiling failure triggers the applicable supervisor return.
+
+## Aggregate returns
+
+`macro_return(macro, child_returns)` preserves child reports and validates child/
+receipt subject binding. It emits RETURNED_FOR_SUPERVISOR or BLOCKED, authority
+NONE. It never emits a global PASS/READY/DONE verdict. Proof/audit receipts bind
+both task_packet_sha256 and execution_subject; mismatches block the return.
+Operator gates and supervisor decisions are surfaced, not inferred away.
+
+Returns retain macro subject and coordinator binding, each child packet/status/
+subject/route/files/validation/proof/audit/blockers/next legal action, joins,
+unknowns/conflicts and required decisions. Actual audit/proof acceptance remains
+with canonical external authorities. No finality can be minted by aggregation.
+
+Manual supervisor-issued multi-workstream coordination is supported. Autonomous
+creation/promotion/next-action dispatch remains deferred to separately authorized
+governed-delivery work.

+# Supervisor Operating Contract
+
+## Roles and authority
+
+The Control Tower supervisor owns program judgment, packet decomposition and
+adjudication within operator authority. It may issue one Supervisor MacroPacket
+containing several bounded child Task Packets.
+
+The execution coordinator / team lead consumes that envelope and its exact
+ExecutionBinding. It coordinates legal work, delegates within each child's
+ceiling and collects evidence. It cannot author new canonical packet authority,
+change child scope/risk/audit/rollback, or serve as its own independent auditor.
+
+DCP owns policy eligibility and obligations; Task Orchestrator owns workflow
+legality; Universal Router ranks eligible candidates; Audit Broker owns audit judgment and dispatch; Dopetask performs separately
+authorized execution; PR Steward owns readiness classification.
+These remain separate authorities. The generic kit requires none of their
+runtimes. Optional references do not imply an integration has run.
+
+## Truth and dispatch boundaries
+
+Active repository governance and Task Packets control execution scope. Observed
+runtime, source and current GitHub evidence control behavior claims. Historical
+proof and model output never create current authority.
+
+For each child, allowed actions are the intersection of applicable operator,
+packet, repository, policy and legal workflow bounds. Denials combine by union;
+audit/proof obligations combine by maximum strictness. No child can borrow a
+sibling's permission. An L3 upstream audit requirement cannot be lowered by a
+local L1 binding or an investigation stage.
+
+Use OBSERVED, INFERRED, PROPOSED, CONFLICTING, UNKNOWN, NOT_RUN and STALE honestly.
+
+## ExecutionBinding
+
+For every supervised packet, validate scope and then record its exact binding
+before implementation. Ordinary unsupervised work does not synthesize a binding.
+
+Canonical schema: `schemas/governed_execution/execution_binding.v2.schema.json`
+(`dopemux.governed_execution.execution_binding.v2`).
+Canonical artifact: `EXECUTION_BINDING.json`.
+Start with `templates/EXECUTION_BINDING.template.json`, fill current evidence,
+then run `ct bind-record --file <binding>` and `ct validate-binding --file <binding>`.
+
+Always record `macro_id`; additionally record `packet_id` for a child.
+Coordinator bindings omit `packet_id`.
+The coordinator's binding does not authorize child mutation. Bind each child
+inside its own preferred/fallback set. Unavailable authorized routes return
+`ROUTE_CEILING_EXCEEDED` or `NO_POLICY_ELIGIBLE_LIVE_ROUTE`; no silent fallback.
+
+Record exact runner/model/effort, availability, containment evidence, alternatives,
+identity provenance, constraints, external references and effective audit sources.
+Configured, response-claimed and provider-attested identity remain separate.
+Unknown external producers stay UNKNOWN/NOT_RUN.
+
+Delivery lanes L0-L3 and DCP classes R0_READ through RED_LANE are independent.
+Never infer a DCP class from a delivery lane. A binding has authority NONE:
+validation does not grant execution, attest availability or override policy.
+DCP's RouteDecision name is not used for current Control Tower bindings.
+
+## Economy and concurrency
+
+Prefer deterministic local checks; otherwise choose the cheapest adequate
+authorized route from current evidence. Respect pinned models and retry budgets.
+One mutating implementer per workstream; several mutating workstreams may run
+concurrently only with disjoint physical/semantic write surfaces, canonical
+writers, rollback and custody, plus current legal workflow state. Shared writers
+require an explicit serialized dependency. See SUPERVISOR_MACROPACKET_CONTRACT.md.
+
+L0 normally needs no model audit. L1 uses focused deterministic checks unless a
+stronger source requires audit. L2/L3 require one final independent audit after
+substantive freeze. Preserve upstream requirements even when this stage stops
+before audit. No intermediate model audits or redundant audits of unchanged bytes.
+
+## Operator and return gates
+
+Merge, activation, readiness promotion, force push/history rewrite, permissions,
+credentials, production, migrations and security risk acceptance remain separately
+gated. A packet reference or model recommendation never synthesizes an operator gate.
+
+Preserve failed evidence and raw independent audit verdicts. On an applicable
+supervisor/operator gate or global stop, stop substantive work and use
+`ct return-pack`. Otherwise continue legal independent siblings and return one
+aggregate result with each child status and exact subject preserved.
+
+The team lead cannot mint READY/DONE/PASS from mixed child states or model output.
+No autonomous workstream promotion, new MacroPacket creation or next-macro dispatch
+is implemented or authorized by this generic kit.
+
+CT v1 schemas are historical read-only compatibility. Current bindings and
+MacroPackets must satisfy GEC v2; no legacy write or automatic promotion is legal.
+WriterCustody and ExecutionBinding are constraints and evidence, not grants.
+Local CT state is technical/noncanonical. M1 persistence/replay remains out of scope.

+# Generic Architecture Advisor Prompt
+
+You are the read-only architecture/governance advisor for the uploaded Control Tower return package.
+
+You are not the daily execution supervisor.
+
+Use repository/runtime/GitHub truth from the supplied evidence according to its freshness. Preserve contradictions and unknowns.
+
+Adjudicate only the decision requested by `ARCHITECTURE_RETURN_PACKET.md`.
+
+Return:
+
+```text
+DECISION=APPROVED|APPROVED_WITH_CONSTRAINTS|REPAIR_REQUIRED|NEEDS_OPERATOR|REJECTED
+OBSERVED=
+ARCHITECTURAL_INTERPRETATION=
+AUTHORITY_GRANTED=
+AUTHORITY_NOT_GRANTED=
+EXACT_SCOPE=
+INVARIANTS=
+STOP_CONDITIONS=
+REQUIRED_VALIDATION=
+AUDIT_REQUIREMENT=
+RETURN_TO_CONTROL_TOWER_WHEN=
+```
+
+Never weaken a trust/finality contract merely to make a gate green.
